Third-party Rust dependency notices

Generated with cargo-about 0.9.2 from Cargo.lock. Do not edit this file.
Regenerate with packaging/generate-notices.sh after dependency changes.

This is the combined dependency set for Wren and wren-relay on Linux x86_64,
macOS ARM64 and Windows x86_64, including build dependencies. An individual
binary uses a subset of these works. Font and icon notices are listed in
THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md and accompany this file in every distribution.

Where a dependency offers alternative licenses, the text below records the
license selected by about.toml. Each distinct upstream license text retains
its copyright notices. Sources for registry crates are available at
https://crates.io/crates/NAME/VERSION, using the names and versions below.
Git sources and revisions are listed beside the corresponding crate.

Licenses for support code
-------------------------

Parts of the TLS test suite are under the Go license. This code is not included
in BoringSSL (i.e. libcrypto and libssl) when compiled, however, so

#ifndef OPENSSL_HEADER_EC_ECP_NISTZ384_H
#define OPENSSL_HEADER_EC_ECP_NISTZ384_H

#include "../../limbs/limbs.h"

#define P384_LIMBS (384u / LIMB_BITS)

typedef struct {
  Limb X[P384_LIMBS];
  Limb Y[P384_LIMBS];
  Limb Z[P384_LIMBS];
} P384_POINT;

typedef struct {
  Limb X[P384_LIMBS];
  Limb Y[P384_LIMBS];
} P384_POINT_AFFINE;


#endif // OPENSSL_HEADER_EC_ECP_NISTZ384_H

// CONNECTION WITH THE USE OR PERFORMANCE OF THIS SOFTWARE.

//! EdDSA Signatures.

use super::ops::ELEM_LEN;
use crate::digest;

pub mod signing;
pub mod verification;

/// The length of an Ed25519 public key.
pub const ED25519_PUBLIC_KEY_LEN: usize = ELEM_LEN;

pub fn eddsa_digest(signature_r: &[u8], public_key: &[u8], msg: &[u8]) -> digest::Digest {
    let mut ctx = digest::Context::new(&digest::SHA512);
    ctx.update(signature_r);
    ctx.update(public_key);
    ctx.update(msg);
    ctx.finish()
}

use crate::limb::Limb;

#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
#[repr(transparent)]
pub struct N0([Limb; 2]);

impl N0 {
    #[cfg(feature = "alloc")]
    pub(super) const LIMBS_USED: usize = 64 / crate::limb::LIMB_BITS;

    #[inline]
    pub const fn precalculated(n0: u64) -> Self {
        #[cfg(target_pointer_width = "64")]
        {
            Self([n0, 0])
        }

        #[cfg(target_pointer_width = "32")]
        {
            Self([n0 as Limb, (n0 >> crate::limb::LIMB_BITS) as Limb])
        }
    }
}

use crate::error::{KeyRejected, Unspecified};

impl From<untrusted::EndOfInput> for Unspecified {
    fn from(source: untrusted::EndOfInput) -> Self {
        super::erase(source)
    }
}

impl From<core::array::TryFromSliceError> for Unspecified {
    fn from(source: core::array::TryFromSliceError) -> Self {
        super::erase(source)
    }
}

impl From<KeyRejected> for Unspecified {
    fn from(source: KeyRejected) -> Self {
        super::erase(source)
    }
}

The goals of the Open Font License (OFL) are to stimulate worldwide

copyright statement(s).
